# Six-tier target schema disposition (gvzkr)

Date: 2026-08-31.

This is a generated re-render of the canonical target schema after the
2026-08-30 purge slice landed. The denominator is produced by fresh
`initialize_archive_tier` execution and includes every declared SQLite
table, column, index, trigger, and view exactly once. It does not read or
mutate the operator archive. The machine-readable source is
`docs/evidence/schema-disposition-2026-08-31.json`.

Code head used: `d30f247af313e91c87f29d749a3756c49c57a58b`.

The current target has no PURGE rows because the historical source receipt
families and index sidecar-purge receipt are already absent from the fresh
target after the landed purge slice. `raw_failure_disposition_receipts`
remains KEEP as a live consumer. `history_sidecars` and `otlp_spans` remain
KEEP; the former is part of blob-liveness authority and the latter remains
a declared telemetry capability.

## Candidate transition binding

The read-only runtime probe found one active index generation and no inactive
candidate generation. Consequently this render records the target schema
and its durable-tier policies but does not claim a candidate-bound user,
audit, embeddings, or ops transition plan. That plan remains a required
successor render once an exact inactive candidate receipt exists; it must
bind predecessor/candidate identities, backups, schema fingerprints, and
the reversible user transition before activation.
